2984 — pistol squat to box

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2984 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A single-leg squat down to a box. The box gives you a target for depth as you build strength.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand in front of a box with one leg extended forward. Brace your core and reach your arms forward for balance. Sit back and down slowly on the standing leg until you touch the box.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2984.json#/instructions/0, 2984.json#/instructions/1, 2984.json#/instructions/2

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 2984.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand in front of a box with one leg extended forward. 2984.json#/instructions/0
  2. Brace your core and reach your arms forward for balance. 2984.json#/instructions/1
  3. Sit back and down slowly on the standing leg until you touch the box. 2984.json#/instructions/2
  4. Drive through your heel to stand. 2984.json#/instructions/3
  5. Finish the reps, then switch legs. 2984.json#/instructions/4

2986 — weighted hammer grip pull up on dip cage

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2986 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: hanging.

A weighted pull-up with a neutral hammer grip on a dip cage. It builds the lats and arms with extra load.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Attach a belt or vest with weight and grip the neutral handles of the cage. Hang with your arms straight and your core braced.

  2. Hang with your arms straight and your core braced. 2986.json#/instructions/1

  5. Lower slowly to a full hang and repeat. 2986.json#/instructions/4

2987 — weighted close grip chin-up on dip cage

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2987 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: blocked_on_source_resolution. Pose class: standing.

A weighted close-grip chin-up on a dip cage. Builds the lats, biceps and upper back.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand in front of the dip cage and grab the parallel bars with an underhand grip, hands shoulder-width apart. Hang from the bars with your arms fully extended, feet off the ground, and body straight.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2987.json#/instructions/0, 2987.json#/instructions/1

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 2987.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand in front of the dip cage and grab the parallel bars with an underhand grip, hands shoulder-width apart. 2987.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hang from the bars with your arms fully extended, feet off the ground, and body straight. 2987.json#/instructions/1
  3. Engage your back muscles and pull your body up towards the bars, keeping your elbows close to your sides. 2987.json#/instructions/2
  4. Continue pulling until your chin is above the bars, then pause for a moment. 2987.json#/instructions/3
  5. Slowly lower your body back down to the starting position, fully extending your arms. 2987.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 2987.json#/instructions/5

2988 — squat mobility

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2988 after decompression) · Source record

A deep squat hold and rock that opens the hips and ankles.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with feet slightly wider than shoulder width.

2989 — squat mobility complex

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2989 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A short flow of deep squat positions that opens up the hips, knees and ankles. Good as a warm-up before leg work.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet a little wider than shoulder width and toes turned out slightly.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2989.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 2989.json#/instructions/0

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2989.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: no additional prop is established. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 2989.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet a little wider than shoulder width and toes turned out slightly. 2989.json#/instructions/0
  2. Sink into a deep squat and keep your heels on the floor. 2989.json#/instructions/1
  3. Press your elbows against your knees to open the hips and hold for a moment. 2989.json#/instructions/2
  4. Shift gently from side to side, then rock forward and back. 2989.json#/instructions/3
  5. Stand up tall and repeat the flow for 6-8 rounds. 2989.json#/instructions/4
  6. Keep your chest lifted and your back long throughout. 2989.json#/instructions/5

2990 — barbell walk calves activation

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2990 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Walking on the balls of your feet with a barbell on your back to wake up the calves before heavy lower body training.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Rest a light barbell across your upper back and hold it with both hands. Stand tall with your core braced and rise onto the balls of your feet.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2990.json#/instructions/0, 2990.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Rest a light barbell across your upper back and hold it with both hands.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2990.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Stand tall with your core braced and rise onto the balls of your feet.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2990.json#/instructions/1

For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 2990.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Rest a light barbell across your upper back and hold it with both hands. 2990.json#/instructions/0
  2. Stand tall with your core braced and rise onto the balls of your feet. 2990.json#/instructions/1
  3. Walk forward in small, controlled steps without letting your heels drop. 2990.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep your ankles steady and your hips level. 2990.json#/instructions/3
  5. Walk 10-15 metres, rest, then repeat for a few rounds. 2990.json#/instructions/4
  6. Use a light load so you can stay high on your toes. 2990.json#/instructions/5

2991 — rollball seated single leg shoulder flexor depressor retractor

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2991 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A seated shoulder control drill on a ball with one leg lifted. It challenges balance while you raise, lower and pull back the shoulder blades.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit tall on a stability ball with your feet on the floor.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2991.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Rest relaxed hands beside the thighs until the source's arm movement begins; use documented handles or loads when present.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 2991.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Sit tall on a stability ball with your feet on the floor.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 2991.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit tall on a stability ball with your feet on the floor. 2991.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lift one foot slightly off the ground and hold your balance. 2991.json#/instructions/1
  3. Raise your arms forward, then press your shoulders down away from your ears. 2991.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pull your shoulder blades back and together without arching your back. 2991.json#/instructions/3
  5. Return slowly to the start and complete your reps. 2991.json#/instructions/4
  6. Switch legs and repeat on the other side. 2991.json#/instructions/5

2992 — calf self massage with barbell

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2992 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A self-massage for the calves using a barbell to roll over tight spots.

Proposed staging

3122 — resistance band seated shoulder press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3122 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A seated shoulder press using a resistance band. Builds the shoulders and triceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on a chair or bench with your back straight and feet flat on the ground. Hold the resistance band with both hands, palms facing forward, and bring it up to shoulder level.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on a chair or bench with your back straight and feet flat on the ground. 3122.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hold the resistance band with both hands, palms facing forward, and bring it up to shoulder level. 3122.json#/instructions/1
  3. Press the band overhead, extending your arms fully. 3122.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower the band back down to shoulder level. 3122.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3122.json#/instructions/4

3123 — resistance band seated biceps curl

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3123 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A seated biceps curl using a resistance band. Builds the biceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on a chair or bench with your back straight and feet flat on the ground. Hold the resistance band with an underhand grip, palms facing up, and arms extended down by your sides.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3123.json#/instructions/0, 3123.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on a chair or bench with your back straight and feet flat on the ground. 3123.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hold the resistance band with an underhand grip, palms facing up, and arms extended down by your sides. 3123.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keeping your upper arms stationary, exhale and curl the resistance band up towards your shoulders. 3123.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment at the top, squeezing your biceps. 3123.json#/instructions/3
  5. Inhale and slowly lower the resistance band back to the starting position. 3123.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3123.json#/instructions/5

A seated chest press using a resistance band. Builds the chest, shoulders and triceps.

Starting Position

A push-up finished with an extra shove that spreads the shoulder blades at the top. It trains the chest and the serratus muscles that stabilise the shoulder blades.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Start in a high plank position with your hands slightly wider than shoulder-width apart and your body in a straight line from head to heels.

Complete ordered source instructions

  2. Lower your body towards the ground by bending your elbows, keeping them close to your sides. 3145.json#/instructions/1

  5. Pause for a moment, then reverse the movement by retracting your shoulder blades and lowering your body back down to the starting position. 3145.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3145.json#/instructions/5

  • Pause for a moment, then reverse the movement by retracting your shoulder blades and lowering your body back down to the starting position. Step 5; 3145.json#/instructions/4

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A standing one arm row from a hip hinge, pulling a weight toward your chest one side at a time. Good for the upper back and lats.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, knees slightly bent, and hold a dumbbell in one hand.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, knees slightly bent, and hold a dumbbell in one hand. 3162.json#/instructions/0
  2. Bend forward at the hips, keeping your back straight and your core engaged. 3162.json#/instructions/1
  3. Let the dumbbell hang straight down in front of you, with your arm fully extended. 3162.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pull the dumbbell up towards your chest, keeping your elbow close to your body. 3162.json#/instructions/3
  5. Squeeze your shoulder blades together at the top of the movement. 3162.json#/instructions/4
  6. Lower the dumbbell back down to the starting position. 3162.json#/instructions/5
  7. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions, then switch sides. 3162.json#/instructions/6

3165 — bodyweight standing row (with towel)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3165 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A standing row done with both hands on a towel, pulling toward your chest. It trains the upper back and shoulder blade squeeze with no gear.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold a towel in front of you with both hands.

Complete ordered source instructions

  2. Bend your knees slightly and hinge forward at the hips, keeping your back straight. 3165.json#/instructions/1
  3. Pull the towel towards your chest, squeezing your shoulder blades together. 3165.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly release the tension and return to the starting position. 3165.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3165.json#/instructions/4

3167 — bodyweight squatting row (with towel)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3167 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: prone.

A squat combined with a towel row, so legs and upper back work together. You pull the towel toward your chest as you sink down.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a towel in front of you with your palms facing down.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 3167.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a towel in front of you with your palms facing down. 3167.json#/instructions/0
  2. Bend your knees and lower your body into a squat position, keeping your back straight and your chest up. 3167.json#/instructions/1
  3. As you lower into the squat, simultaneously pull the towel towards your chest, squeezing your shoulder blades together. 3167.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment at the bottom of the squat, then slowly return to the starting position while extending your arms. 3167.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3167.json#/instructions/4

A dumbbell fly with your feet lifted and knees at 90 degrees. It stretches the chest and keeps your back flat.

Lie on a bench holding dumbbells above your chest.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Lie on a bench holding dumbbells above your chest. 3175.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lift your feet so your knees are bent to 90 degrees. 3175.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keep a slight bend in your elbows. 3175.json#/instructions/2
  4. Open your arms out wide until you feel a chest stretch. 3175.json#/instructions/3
  5. Bring the dumbbells back together over your chest. 3175.json#/instructions/4
  6. Move slowly and keep your back flat. 3175.json#/instructions/5

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Adjust the glute-ham raise machine to fit your body. 3193.json#/instructions/0
  2. Position yourself face down on the machine with your ankles secured. 3193.json#/instructions/1
  3. Place your hands on your chest or cross them over your chest. 3193.json#/instructions/2
  4. Engage your hamstrings and glutes to lift your upper body up towards the ceiling. 3193.json#/instructions/3
  5. Continue lifting until your body is in a straight line from your head to your heels. 3193.json#/instructions/4
  6. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower your body back down to the starting position. 3193.json#/instructions/5
  7. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3193.json#/instructions/6
